Executive Summary\n6: \n7: **Browser Use** and **Computer Use** have evolved from brittle DOM selector scripts into autonomous visual-language agents capable of perceiving desktop screens and web pages like humans. \n8: \n9: Agents now combine **Vision-Language Models (VLMs)**, **Model Context Protocol (MCP)**, **Accessibility Tree (a11y) parsing**, and **pixel coordinate grounding** to fill forms, navigate multi-step flows, execute system commands, and bypass dynamic UI changes.\n10: \n11: ---\n12: \n13: ## 2. Master Comparison Matrix: Open-Source vs. Paid Tools\n14: \n15: | Project / Tool | Type | Focus | License / Access | Model Compatibility | Key Strength |\n16: | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |\n17: | **`browser-use`** | Open Source Library | Python LLM Browser Automation | MIT | OpenRouter, Ollama, OpenAI, Anthropic | Dominant OSS browser agent framework. Highly extensible. |\n18: | **`UI-TARS` (ByteDance)** | Open Weights & Desktop App | GUI Agent Model & OS Execution | Apache 2.0 | Native UI-TARS VLM backbone | Top benchmark performer on OSWorld & Windows Arena. |\n19: | **`Agent-S` (Simular AI)** | Open Source Framework | Cross-OS Desktop & Web Agent | Apache 2.0 | OpenAI, Anthropic, Qwen2.5-VL | Human-like OS control across Windows, Mac, Linux. |\n20: | **`Stagehand` (Browserbase)** | Open Source SDK | Playwright-native `act/extract/observe` | MIT | OpenAI, Anthropic, Custom LLMs | Surgical precision web automation with DOM + Vision hybrid. |\n21: | **`Midscene.js`** | Open Source SDK | Visual UI Automation for Web & Mobile | MIT | OpenAI, Qwen-VL, Claude | Vision-first UI automation operating via screenshots. |\n22: | **`Skyvern`** | Open Source Engine | Production Web Automation Engine | AGPLv3 | OpenAI, Anthropic, Local LLMs | Computer vision + LLM workflow runner with cloud option. |\n23: | **`AgentQL`** | Developer API & Query SDK | Semantic Natural Language Web Querying | Proprietary API / Free Tier | Hosted AgentQL Backend | Replaces fragile XPath/CSS selectors with semantic queries. |\n24: | **`Steel Browser`** | Open Source Infra | Agent-First Headless Browser API | Apache 2.0 | Agnostic | Cloud/Self-hosted browser infra with CAPTCHA/session management. |\n25: | **`Anthropic Computer Use`** | Proprietary API | Native Desktop Computer Control | API Key | Claude 3.5 Sonnet / 3.7 Sonnet | Native API tool definitions (`computer_20241022`). |\n26: | **`OpenInterpreter`** | Open Source | Terminal, Code & Desktop GUI Agent | MIT | Any LLM (Ollama, OpenRouter, GPT-4o) | Conversational computer control in terminal & desktop. |\n27: | **`OpenClaw`** | Open Source | Personal AI Assistant & Agent Skills | MIT | Agnostic | Autonomous local agent with browser + shell skills. |\n28: \n29: ---\n30: \n31: ## 3. Essential \"Awesome\" Lists & Repositories to Bookmark\n32: \n33: 1. **[awesome-web-agents](https://github.com/steel-dev/awesome-web-agents)** (Steel.dev)\n34:    - The definitive index for web agents, cloud browsers, browser extensions, and computer-use tools.\n35: 2. **[acu — Awesome Computer Use](https://github.com/trycua/acu)** (CUA)\n36:    - Papers, datasets, frameworks, OS drivers, and desktop benchmarks.\n37: 3. **[awesome-autonomous-web](https://github.com/Agent-Tools/awesome-autonomous-web)**\n38:    - Curated list of agent browsers, Stagehand, browser-use, Skyvern, and MCP browser tools.\n39: 4. **[Awesome-Long-Horizon-Agents](https://github.com/RUC-NLPIR/Awesome-Long-Horizon-Agents)**\n40:    - Research papers and code for complex multi-step GUI and computer action agents.\n41: 5. **[GUI-Agents-Paper-List](https://github.com/OSU-NLP-Group/GUI-Agents-Paper-List)** (OSU NLP)\n42:    - Benchmark tracker and academic literature for computer/web GUI agents.\n43: 6. **[awesome-playwright](https://github.com/mxschmitt/awesome-playwright)**\n44:    - Playwright ecosystem tools with dedicated AI & Agents section.\n45: \n46: ---\n47: \n48: ## 4. Best AI Models for Computer & Browser Use\n49: \n50: ### A. Top Free & Open-Weights Models (Self-Hostable or via OpenRouter Free)\n51: \n52: 1. **Qwen2.5-VL (7B & 72B)** (*Alibaba Cloud*)\n53:    - **Why it leads**: Native visual grounding, dynamic resolution parsing, precise bounding box and point coordinate detection. Automation Developers  \n5: > **Last Updated**: July 2026  \n6: \n7: ---\n8: \n9: ## Executive Summary & Landscape Map\n10: \n11: The domain of **Browser Use** and **Computer Use** has evolved from Primary Browser Use & Computer Use Projects\n41: \n42: ### 1.1 Browser Use (`browser-use/browser-use`)\n43: * **Core Language**: Python (with Rust core modules for high-performance DOM parsing & tree extraction).\n44: * **Architecture**: Connects LLMs directly to browser pages via Playwright and Chrome DevTools Protocol (CDP).
